Let's think about coordinate line search implementation in detail.

Function `sequence_score(a)` uses FFT to compute c, S. But in coordinate descent maintain c.

Function `update_c_for_coord(c, a, j, new_val)`:
- old = a[j]; delta = new_val - old.
- if abs(delta)<tiny: return c.
- m=len(c)=2n-1.
- s = zeros(m); s[j:j+n] = a[:n] (normalized old)
- c_new = c + 2*delta*s
- if 0 <= 2*j < m: c_new[2*j] += delta*delta
But wait if a_j changed, the base a in s should be old a_j? The term 2Δ x^j P includes 2Δ a_j at k=2j. Plus Δ². If P included old a_j; expansion okay. For k=2j, 2Δ a_old + Δ² = 2oldΔ + Δ² = new²-old², correct.
But what about when s[j+j]=old a_j. Good.
- Then normalize by q² where q=S0/(S0+delta). c_new *= q*q.
Return c_new.
Note: If we search v from base, `delta` can be negative; s old a. Good.

Golden section line search for v:

def coordinate_line_search(a, c, j, n_evals=25):
    n=a.size; m=2*n-1; S0=1.0
    old=float(a[j]); avg=1.0/n
    hi = max(old*3.0 + 2.0*avg, 20.0*avg)
    if hi <= 0: hi=20*avg
    hi = min(hi, 10.0) # cap
    s = zeros(m); s[j:j+n]=a
    tmp = empty(m)
    # local eval inline via helper function? Can't nest. We can define top-level `_coord_eval(v, old, c, s, tmp, m, k2)` but it will allocate? top-level okay.
```
Rules: no closures from function nesting, no lambdas. Top-level helper can be used. `_coord_eval` takes many args and returns. But it will modify tmp; okay. It also uses `inf`? Fine. To avoid function call overhead inside golden (25 calls) negligible.
```
def _coord_eval(v, old, c, s, tmp, k2):
    d = v-old
    if abs(d) < 1e-15:
        max_raw = c.max()
    else:
        np.multiply(s, 2.0*d, out=tmp)
        np.add(c, tmp, out=tmp)
        if 0 <= k2 < tmp.size:
            tmp[k2] += d*d
        max_raw = tmp.max()
    newsum = 1.0 + d  # since S0=1
    if newsum <= 1e-15: return inf
    q = 1.0/newsum
    return max_raw*q*q
```
But `tmp` is modified; next eval multiply overwrites all elements? `np.multiply(s, factor, out=tmp)` writes all elements, yes. Then add c. Good.
We need c.max each eval if d=0; c base max precompute? Could use `cmax_base` to avoid max. Pass. If d=0, max_raw=cmax_base.

`coordinate_descent(a_in, budget, t_start, initial_topk=80, max_sweeps=5)`:
```

Resampling function:
```
def resample_length(a, target):
    n=a.size
    if target==n: return a.copy()
    x_old=np.arange(n); x_new=np.linspace(0,n-1,target)
    b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    return normalize_sum1(b)
```
This smooths; then coordinate can adjust.

Detailed functions:

`linear_conv(a)`:
```
def linear_conv(a):
    n = int(np.size(a))
    m = 2*n - 1
    nfft = 1
    while nfft < m: nfft <<=1
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, nfft)
    return c[:m].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
```
Could precompute nfft in optimize and pass to avoid recomputing. But for function simplicity, compute. In Adam, calling many times; compute nfft each call? We can pass but top-level function maybe overhead. We'll implement `conv_fft(a, nfft=None)` and compute if None. In loops, maintain.

`correlate_pa(p, a, hfft=None)`:
```
def correlate_pa(p, a, hfft=None):
    n = a.size; m=2n-1
    if hfft is None: L=1 while L < m+n-1: L<<=1
    RA=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], hfft)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p, hfft)
    h=np.fft.irfft(RA*P,hfft)
    return h[n-1:2*n-1]
```
In Adam, p length m, a length n.

def coordinate_descent(a_in, budget, t_start, max_top=300, verbose=True):
    a = normalize_sum1(a_in)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2: return 2.0, a
    c = linear_conv(a)
    best_score = 2.0*n*c.max()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    deadline = t_start + budget
    top_k = 40
    evals = 30
    sweep = 0
    no_improve_sweeps =0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
        # recompute c from best_a? Use best_a to avoid drift? We operate on current a. At start of sweep, recompute from a.
        c = linear_conv(a)
        cur_score = 2.0*n*c.max()
        if cur_score < best_score: best_score=cur_score; best_a=a.copy()
        # adjust top_k/evals with time
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 20: break
        # top_k increases each sweep but not exceed max_top and n
        top_k = min(max_top, n, int(top_k * 1.5) + 20)
        # evals decrease if n large/time small
        if n > 8000: evals = min(evals, 18)
        elif n > 2000: evals = min(evals, 25)
        else: evals = min(evals, 35)
        selected = select_coordinates(a,c,top_k)
        improved=0
        for j in selected:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
            res = coordinate_line_search(a,c,int(j),evals)
            if res is not None:
                a,c,score = res
                if score < best_score:
                    best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); improved+=1
                else:
                    # still accept if score < cur_score + tolerance? We want keep best. If line search returns only if lower than base, score should < base_score. But base_score may >best? If a is worse than best, score could be less than base but still >best. We should update a? If a current is not best, we may want to follow improves. We maintain a as current sequence, not necessarily best. At start of each sweep, maybe set a=best_a? To only work on best. Simpler: At start coordinate_descent, and after each coordinate if score > best? We can reset a to best_a? If line search improves current but current remains worse than best, no need. Let's always operate on best_a: at start set a=best_a; after each accepted update, if score < best, update best; since accepted only if score<base, and base=best initially, a remains best. If some accepted not improve best? base=best, so accepted always improves best. Good. At start of each sweep, set a=best_a.copy(), c=linear_conv(best_a). Then any accepted improves best. This avoids drifting worse.

`select_coordinates` code no rng to be deterministic. Could include random by passing rng; but top-level no closure. We can create `select_coordinates(a,c,top_k, seed=None)`.
```
def select_coordinates(a,c,top_k, seed=None):
   n=len(a)
   if top_k>=n: return np.arange(n)
   top_peaks = np.argsort(c)[-min(8,c.size):]
   peak_shift=np.zeros(n)
   for k in top_peaks:
      k=int(k); lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(k,n-1)
      if hi>=lo:
         idx=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
         peak_shift[idx] += a[k-idx]
   rank=peak_shift + 0.2*a
   order=np.argsort(rank)[::-1]
   top_a=np.argsort(a)[::-1]
   seen=np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
   selected=[]
   for idx in order:
      idx=int(idx)
      if not seen[idx]: seen[idx]=True; selected.append(idx); if len(selected)>=top_k: break
   for idx in top_a:
      idx=int(idx)
      if not seen[idx]: ...
      if len(selected)>=int(top_k*1.5): break
   if seed is not None:
      # add random few
      for _ in range(min(10, top_k//5)):
         idx=int(rng.integers(n))? But seed default_rng each call? Could be overhead. Skip.
   return np.array(selected[:top_k*2], dtype=int)
```
We can return up to 1.5 top_k; coordinate loop maybe more. Fine.

```
def _coord_eval(v, old, c, s, tmp, k2):
    d = v - old
    if abs(d) < 1e-15:
        max_raw = float(c.max())
    else:
        factor = 2.0*d
        np.multiply(s, factor, out=tmp)
        np.add(c, tmp, out=tmp)
        if 0 <= k2 < tmp.size:
            tmp[k2] += d*d
        max_raw = float(tmp.max())
    newsum = 1.0 + d
    if newsum <= 1e-15:
        return math.inf
    q = 1.0 / newsum
    return max_raw * q * q
```
But if d negative large and newsum 0, inf. ok.

       else: final_tmp = c.copy()

       a_new = a * q
       a_new[j] = best_v * q

       sn = a_new.sum()
       if sn <= 1e-15: return None
       a_new /= sn
       c_new /= (sn*sn)
       # if c_new has tiny negative from FFT? raw exact nonnegative, but numerical; max no neg. Use np.maximum.
       c_new = np.maximum(c_new, 0.0)
       score = 2.0*n*c_new.max()
       if score < base_score - 1e-12:
           return a_new,c_new,score
       else:
           # fallback to best? no
    return None

def mixed_direction(a_in, budget, t_start):

    best_b=b.copy(); best_score=...

    while time < deadline-5:
       it+=1
       z = logb
       e=np.exp(z-z.max()); b=e/e.sum()
       B=np.fft.rfft(b,nfft)
       y=np.fft.irfft(A_fft*B,nfft)[:m]
       ymax=y.max()

       p=np.exp(scaled); p/=p.sum()

       logb -= eta*g
       logb -= np.mean(logb)
       if it % 20 ==0:
           score=... b
           if score<best: best_b=b
    return best_score,best_b

Implement `perturb_spectral(a, rng, strength)`:
```
def perturb_spectral(a, rng, strength=0.25):
   n=a.size; m=2n-1; nfft=next pow
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
   amp = 1.0 + strength*rng.standard_normal(A.size)
   amp=np.clip(amp,0.3,2.0)
   phase=strength*0.5*rng.standard_normal(A.size)
   A2=A*amp*np.exp(1j*phase)
   b=np.fft.irfft(A2,nfft)[:n]
   b=np.maximum(b,0); if sum>0 normalize else return a
```

Potential top-level helper `next_pow2(x)`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    p=1
    while p < m: p <<=1
    return p
```

`normalize_sum1`:
```
def normalize_sum1(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
       return np.ones_like(a)/max(1,a.size)
    a /= s
    return a
```

`conv_fft`:
```
def conv_fft(a, nfft=None):
    n=a.size; m=2*n-1
    if nfft is None: nfft=next_pow2(m)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,nfft)
    return c[:m]
```

`conv_score_with_c`:
```
def conv_score_with_c(a):
   n=a.size; c=conv_fft(a); return 2n*c.max(), c
```

`z_from_a`, `softmax_z`? We'll implement inside adam but top-level:
```
def z_from_a(a):
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-300)
   z=np.log(a)
   zmax=z.max()
   s=np.exp(z-zmax).sum()
   return z - (zmax + np.log(s))
```
`softmax_z_to_probs(z)`:
```
def softmax_probs(z):
   zmax=z.max(); e=np.exp(z-zmax); return e/e.sum()
```

`adam_lse_refine` will use these.

Let's write `adam_lse_refine` fully:
```
def adam_lse_refine(a_in, budget, t_start):
    a0=np.asarray(a_in,dtype=np.float64)
    if a0.size<1: return 2.0, np.ones(1)
    a=normalize_sum1(a0)
    n=a.size
    c0=conv_fft(a); best_score=2.0*n*c0.max(); best_a=a.copy()
    deadline=t_start+float(budget)
    if n < 16 or budget < 5: return best_score,best_a
    m_len=2*n-1
    nfft=next_pow2(m_len); hfft=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    betas=[]
    for bd in (0.05,0.2,0.8,3.0,12.0,48.0):
        betas.append(float(n)*bd)
    if budget < 60: betas=betas[-2:]
    elif budget < 200: betas=betas[-4:]
    z=z_from_a(best_a)
    m_adam=np.zeros(n); v_adam=np.zeros(n); step=0
    b1=0.9; b2=0.999; eps=1e-8; lr=0.02
    time_per_beta=max(5.0, float(budget)/len(betas))
    for idx,beta in enumerate(betas):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 5: break
        end_t=min(deadline-2, time.monotonic()+time_per_beta)
        no_improve=0
        while time.monotonic() < end_t:
            step += 1
            a = softmax_probs(z)
            # small floor to avoid underflow
            # if a.min() < 1e-12: a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12/n); a/=a.sum()
            A=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,nfft)[:m_len]
            cmax=float(c.max()); score=2.0*n*cmax
            if score < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 10000: break
            scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
            # if scaled has NaN? no
            e2=np.exp(scaled); s2=e2.sum()
            if s2 <=0 or not np.isfinite(s2):
                p=np.zeros(m_len); p[int(c.argmax())]=1.0
            else:
                p=e2/s2
            RA=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], hfft); P=np.fft.rfft(p, hfft)
            h=np.fft.irfft(RA*P, hfft)[n-1:2*n-1]
            mu=float(np.dot(a,h))
            grad_z = a*(h-mu)
            # optional damp nan
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_z)):
                grad_z = np.zeros(n)
            m_adam = b1*m_adam + (1-b1)*grad_z
            v_adam = b2*v_adam + (1-b2)*grad_z*grad_z
            m_hat = m_adam/(1-b1**step)
            v_hat = v_adam/(1-b2**step)
            lr_t = lr / (1.0 + 0.0002*step)
            z = z - lr_t * m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat)+eps)
            if step % 20 ==0:
                z=z_from_a(softmax_probs(z)) # rescale from current? expensive but okay every20.
```
`z_from_a(softmax_probs(z))` does log softmax; O(n) and gives stable z. But every 20 steps sequence copy; okay. Could just subtract logsumexp:
```
zmax=z.max(); w=np.exp(z-zmax); z -= (zmax + np.log(w.sum()))
```
Use that.

        if sc_cur < best_score: best...
        if sc_cur > best_score*1.05:
            z=z_from_a(best_a); m_adam.fill(0); v_adam.fill(0); step=0
```
But if reset step, b1**step. okay.
Return best.

Pseudo:
```
def coordinate_descent(a_in, budget, t_start, max_top=300):
   a=normalize_sum1(a_in); n=a.size
   c=conv_fft(a); best_score=...
   best_a=a.copy()
   deadline=t_start+budget
   top_k=50; evals=28; no_improve=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
       c=conv_fft(best_a)  # use best each sweep
       cur=2n*c.max(); if cur<best_score update
       top_k = min(max_top,n, top_k + 50)
       if n>8000: evals=max(12, evals-2)
       selected=select_coordinates(best_a,c,min(top_k,n))
       improved=0
       a=best_a.copy(); c_cur = conv_fft(a)? We already c. Set a=best_a.copy(), c=c.copy()
       for j in selected:
          if time>deadline-10: break
          res=coordinate_line_search(a,c,int(j),evals)
          if res is not None:
             a,c_res,score=res
             if score < best_score - 1e-14:
                best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); c=c_res; improved+=1
       if improved==0:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve>=3: break
       else: no_improve=0
       # If top_k == n, break next? maybe
   return best_score,best_a
```
If `coordinate_line_search` uses `a,c` and returns new c normalized. After update, c corresponds to a. Good.
Potential issue: If coordinate update improves score but due rounding c not exact, next coordinate uses it. Recompute each sweep from best_a avoids drift.

def _pair_eval(d, c, diff, tmp, qidx1,qidx2,k):
   if abs(d)<1e-15: return cmax_base
   np.multiply(diff, 2.0*d, out=tmp)
   np.add(c, tmp, out=tmp)
   if 0<=qidx1<m: tmp[qidx1] += d*d
   if 0<=qidx2<m: tmp[qidx2] += d*d
   if 0<=k<m: tmp[k] -= 2.0*d*d
   return float(tmp.max())
```

We need top-level `select_pairs`: Using Python set for duplicate; top-level can create `seen = set()`.
```
def select_pairs(a,c,max_pairs=40, top_peaks=5):
   n=a.size; m=c.size; pairs=[]; seen=set()
   if m <=2: return pairs
   peak_idx=np.argsort(c)[-min(top_peaks,m):]
   for k in peak_idx:
      k=int(k); lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(k,n-1)
      if hi<lo: continue
      idx=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
      mirror=k-idx
      prod=a[idx]*a[mirror]
      order=np.argsort(prod)[::-1]
      for pos in order[:20]:
         j=int(idx[pos]); l=int(mirror[pos])
         if j==l: continue
         if j>l: j,l = l,j
         key=(j,l)
         if key not in seen:
            seen.add(key); pairs.append(key)
         if len(pairs)>=max_pairs: return pairs
   return pairs
```
If peak k and mirror pair has j+l=k. But if canonical swap, sum still k. In pair_line_search, `k = j+l` (canonical j+l same). Good.

def append_candidate(pool, scores, a, score, limit=8):
   if not np.isfinite(score): return
   if len(pool) < limit:
      pool.append(np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()); scores.append(float(score))
   else:
      # if score lower than max current, replace max
      worst = int(np.argmax(scores))
      if score < scores[worst]: pool[worst]=...; scores[worst]=score
```

Pseudo full:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    total_budget=float(budget_s)
    search_deadline = t_start + max(60.0, total_budget - 60.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent = None
    try:
       raw = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
       if raw is not None:
          arr=np.asarray(raw,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
          arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
          if arr.size>=16 and arr.sum()>1e-12: parent=normalize_sum1(arr)
    except Exception: pass
    if parent is None:
       n0=4096
       base = np.ones(n0)
       base += 0.25*(np.sin(np.linspace(0,3,n0))**2) # nonneg
       parent=normalize_sum1(np.maximum(base,1e-6))
    # starts
    starts=[parent]
    for alpha in (-1.2,-0.5,0.5,1.2): starts.append(tilt_sequence(parent,alpha))
    if parent.size != 4096 and parent.size>=2048:
       starts.append(resample_length(parent, 4096))
    if parent.size < 8192:
       starts.append(resample_length(parent, 8192))
    for st in (0.12,0.25): starts.append(perturb_spectral(parent,rng,st))
    # choose best start
    best_score=inf; best_a=parent
    pool=[]; pool_scores=[]
    for s in starts:
       try:
          sc,c = conv_score_with_c(s)
       except: continue
       if sc < best_score:
          best_score=sc; best_a=s.copy()
       append_candidate(pool, pool_scores, s, sc, 12)
    # main phases
    # phase1 coordinate
    p1_budget = min(300.0, max(60.0, (search_deadline - time.monotonic())*0.20))
    if time.monotonic() < search_deadline - 20:
       sc1,a1 = coordinate_descent(best_a, p1_budget, time.monotonic(), max_top=300)
       if sc1 < best_score: best_score=sc1; best_a=a1; print
       append_candidate(pool, pool_scores, best_a, best_score)
    # phase2 adam
    rem = search_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120:
       adam_budget = min(rem*0.35, max(120.0, 900.0))
       sc2,a2 = adam_lse_refine(best_a, adam_budget, time.monotonic())
       if sc2 < best_score: update
       append
    # diverse quick if time
    rem = search_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 300:
       diverse_budget = min(180.0, rem*0.15)
       # generate candidates from best_a
       candidates=[]
       candidates.append(tilt_sequence(best_a, 0.8))
       candidates.append(tilt_sequence(best_a, -0.8))
       candidates.append(perturb_spectral(best_a, rng, 0.2))
       if best_a.size < 10000:
           candidates.append(resample_length(best_a, int(best_a.size*1.15)))
       elif best_a.size > 5000:
           candidates.append(resample_length(best_a, 8192))
       per = max(20.0, diverse_budget/len(candidates))
       for cand in candidates:
           if time.monotonic() > search_deadline - 120: break
           scd,ad = coordinate_descent(cand, per, time.monotonic(), max_top=150)
           if scd < best_score: update
           append
    # phase3 final coordinate
    rem = search_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30:
       max_top = 800 if best_a.size < 5000 else 400
       sc3,a3 = coordinate_descent(best_a, rem, time.monotonic(), max_top=max_top)
       if sc3 < best_score: update
       append
    # prepare candidates for verification: sort pool by score, best_a first
    if best_a is None: fallback
    # Verify
    final_a=best_a; final_score=best_score
    try:
       if len(pool)>0:
          order=np.argsort(pool_scores)
          verify_count=0
          while verify_count < 5 and time.monotonic() < t_start + total_budget - 10:
             idx=int(order[verify_count]); arr=pool[idx]
             lst=[float(max(0.0,min(1000.0,x))) for x in arr]
             if sum(lst) < 0.01: ...
             scv=float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
             if np.isfinite(scv) and scv < final_score:
                 final_score=scv; final_a=arr
             verify_count +=1
       # Also maybe verify best_a if not in pool? It likely is. Ensure by appendCandidate with best.
    except Exception: pass
    # if final_score inf? use best_a
    out=[float(max(0.0,min(1000.0,x))) for x in final_a]
    # ensure sum
    if not out or sum(out)<0.01: out=[0.5]
    # final verify if possible
    try:
       scv=float(evaluate_sequence(out))
       if not np.isfinite(scv): ... maybe return [1.0/n]*n? But if eval says inf, any valid maybe. Keep.
    except: pass
    print final
    return out
```

`append_candidate` top-level:
```
def append_candidate(pool, scores, a, score, limit=10):
    if a is None or not np.isfinite(score): return
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if arr.size ==0: return
    if len(pool) < limit:
        pool.append(arr); scores.append(float(score))
    else:
        # replace worst
        worst = int(np.argmax(scores))
        if score < scores[worst]:
            pool[worst]=arr; scores[worst]=float(score)
```

```
def _pair_eval(d, c, diff, tmp, q1,q2,k, base_cmax):
   if abs(d)<1e-15: return base_cmax
   np.multiply(diff, 2.0*d, out=tmp)
   np.add(c, tmp, out=tmp)
   if 0 <= q1 < tmp.size: tmp[q1] += d*d
   if 0 <= q2 < tmp.size: tmp[q2] += d*d
   if 0 <= k < tmp.size: tmp[k] -= 2.0*d*d
   return float(tmp.max())
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing

    F(a) = 2*n*max(c) / (sum(a))**2,   c = np.convolve(a, a)

using a hybrid of:
  - smoothed-logsum-exp Adam in a log-parameterization,
  - exact 1-D coordinate minimization with hash update of the convolution,
  - exact 2-D sum-preserving pair moves on active peak conflicts,
  - diversified spectral/tilt/resampling restarts.

The function propose_candidate(seed, budget_s) is the entry point.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math


def next_pow2(x):
    p = 1
    while p < x:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def normalize_sum1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def conv_fft(a, nfft=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if nfft is None:
        nfft = next_pow2(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, nfft)
    return c[:m].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def conv_score_with_c(a):
    n = a.size
    c = conv_fft(a)
    return float(2.0 * n * c.max()), c


def z_from_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    z = np.log(a)
    zm = float(z.max())
    if not np.isfinite(zm):
        return np.zeros_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.exp(z - zm)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.zeros_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
    return z - (zm + math.log(s))


def softmax_probs(z):
    zm = float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(zm):
        z = z - float(np.mean(z)) if np.isfinite(np.mean(z)) else np.zeros_like(z)
        zm = float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z - zm)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def append_candidate(pool, scores, a, score, limit=10):
    if a is None:
        return
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    try:
        sc = float(score)
    except Exception:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    if len(pool) < limit:
        pool.append(arr.copy())
        scores.append(sc)
    else:
        if scores:
            worst = int(np.argmax(scores))
            if sc < scores[worst]:
                pool[worst] = arr.copy()
                scores[worst] = sc


def tilt_sequence(a, alpha):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return normalize_sum1(a)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    x -= float(x.mean())
    b = a * np.exp(alpha * x)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return normalize_sum1(a)
    return b / s


def perturb_spectral(a, rng, strength=0.2):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return normalize_sum1(a)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = next_pow2(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    amp = 1.0 + strength * rng.standard_normal(A.size)
    amp = np.clip(amp, 0.35, 1.9)
    phase = 0.5 * strength * rng.standard_normal(A.size)
    A2 = A * amp * np.exp(1j * phase)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A2, nfft)[:n]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return normalize_sum1(a)
    return b / s


def resample_length(a, target):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    target = int(max(2, target))
    n = a.size
    if target == n:
        return normalize_sum1(a)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.full(target, 1.0 / target, dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, n - 1.0, target, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(target, 1.0 / target, dtype=np.float64)
    return b / s


def adam_lse_refine(a_in, budget, t_start):
    a0 = np.asarray(a_in, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = normalize_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        c = conv_fft(a)
        return float(2.0 * n * c.max()), normalize_sum1(a)

    budget = max(0.0, float(budget))
    if budget < 5.0:
        c = conv_fft(a)
        return float(2.0 * n * c.max()), a

    c0 = conv_fft(a)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * c0.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    deadline = t_start + budget

    M = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = next_pow2(M)
    hfft = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    betas = []
    for bd in (0.05, 0.25, 1.0, 4.0, 16.0, 64.0):
        betas.append(float(max(1.0, bd * n)))

    if budget < 80.0:
        betas = betas[-2:]
    elif budget < 250.0:
        betas = betas[-4:]

    ref_z = z_from_a(best_a)
    z = ref_z.copy()
    mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    step = 0
    b1 = 0.9
    b2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8
    lr = 0.018

    per_beta = max(5.0, budget / float(max(1, len(betas))))

    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 4.0:
            break
        end_beta = min(deadline - 2.0, time.monotonic() + per_beta)
        stall = 0

        while time.monotonic() < end_beta:
            step += 1
            a = softmax_probs(z)

            A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
            c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, nfft)[:M]
            c_max = float(c.max())
            score = float(2.0 * n * c_max)
            if score < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = score
                best_a = a.copy()
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 1

            if stall > 12000:
                break

            scaled = beta * (c - c_max)
            e = np.exp(scaled)
            se = float(e.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
                p = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
                p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
            else:
                p = e / se

            RA = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], hfft)
            P = np.fft.rfft(p, hfft)
            h = np.fft.irfft(RA * P, hfft)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]

            mu = float(np.dot(a, h))
            grad_z = a * (h - mu)

            mom = b1 * mom + (1.0 - b1) * grad_z
            vel = b2 * vel + (1.0 - b2) * grad_z * grad_z
            b1_t = b1 ** step
            b2_t = b2 ** step
            m_hat = mom / (1.0 - b1_t)
            v_hat = vel / (1.0 - b2_t)
            lr_t = lr / (1.0 + 0.00015 * step)
            z = z - lr_t * m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat) + eps)

            if step % 25 == 0:
                zm = float(z.max())
                if not np.isfinite(zm):
                    z = z_from_a(best_a)
                    mom.fill(0.0)
                    vel.fill(0.0)
                    step = 0
                    break
                w = np.exp(z - zm)
                ws = float(w.sum())
                if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
                    z = z_from_a(best_a)
                    mom.fill(0.0)
                    vel.fill(0.0)
                    step = 0
                    break
                z = z - (zm + math.log(ws))
                z = np.clip(z, -100.0, 100.0)

        a_cur = softmax_probs(z)
        c_cur = conv_fft(a_cur)
        sc_cur = float(2.0 * n * c_cur.max())
        if np.isfinite(sc_cur) and sc_cur < best_score:
            best_score = sc_cur
            best_a = a_cur.copy()

        if not np.isfinite(sc_cur) or sc_cur > best_score * 1.08:
            z = z_from_a(best_a)
            mom.fill(0.0)
            vel.fill(0.0)
            step = 0

    a_final = softmax_probs(z)
    c_final = conv_fft(a_final)
    sc_final = float(2.0 * n * c_final.max())
    if np.isfinite(sc_final) and sc_final < best_score:
        best_score = sc_final
        best_a = normalize_sum1(a_final)
    return best_score, best_a


def _coord_eval(v, old, c, s, tmp, k2, base_cmax):
    d = float(v) - float(old)
    if abs(d) < 1e-15:
        max_raw = base_cmax
    else:
        np.multiply(s, 2.0 * d, out=tmp)
        np.add(c, tmp, out=tmp)
        if 0 <= k2 < tmp.size:
            tmp[k2] += d * d
        max_raw = float(tmp.max())
    new_sum = 1.0 + d
    if new_sum <= 1e-12:
        return math.inf
    q = 1.0 / new_sum
    return max_raw * q * q


def coordinate_line_search(a, c, j, n_evals=12):
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return None
    m = 2 * n - 1
    old = float(a[j])
    avg = 1.0 / float(n)

    if old > 10.0 * avg:
        hi = min(1.0, old * 3.0 + 5.0 * avg)
    else:
        hi = min(1.0, max(100.0 * avg, old + 25.0 * avg))
    if hi <= old + 1e-12:
        hi = min(1.0, max(old * 2.0, 25.0 * avg))
    if hi <= 0.0:
        hi = 25.0 * avg

    s = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    s[j:j + n] = a
    tmp = np.empty(m, dtype=np.float64)
    base_cmax = float(c.max())
    k2 = 2 * int(j)

    grid = np.linspace(0.0, max(hi, 1e-15), 9, dtype=np.float64)
    best_v = 0.0
    best_f = math.inf
    best_idx = 0
    for idx, v in enumerate(grid):
        f = _coord_eval(v, old, c, s, tmp, k2, base_cmax)
        if f < best_f:
            best_f = f
            best_v = float(v)
            best_idx = idx

    f_old = _coord_eval(old, old, c, s, tmp, k2, base_cmax)
    if np.isfinite(f_old) and f_old < best_f:
        best_f = f_old
        best_v = old

    if best_idx <= 0:
        lo = 0.0
        hi2 = grid[min(1, len(grid) - 1)]
    elif best_idx >= len(grid) - 1:
        lo = grid[-2]
        hi2 = max(hi, grid[-1])
    else:
        lo = grid[best_idx - 1]
        hi2 = grid[best_idx + 1]
    if hi2 <= lo:
        lo, hi2 = 0.0, hi

    gr = (math.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0
    x1 = hi2 - gr * (hi2 - lo)
    x2 = lo + gr * (hi2 - lo)
    f1 = _coord_eval(x1, old, c, s, tmp, k2, base_cmax)
    f2 = _coord_eval(x2, old, c, s, tmp, k2, base_cmax)

    if f1 < best_f:
        best_f, best_v = f1, float(x1)
    if f2 < best_f:
        best_f, best_v = f2, float(x2)

    iterations = max(2, int(n_evals) - 2)
    for _ in range(iterations):
        if f1 < f2:
            hi2 = x2
            x2 = x1
            f2 = f1
            x1 = hi2 - gr * (hi2 - lo)
            f1 = _coord_eval(x1, old, c, s, tmp, k2, base_cmax)
        else:
            lo = x1
            x1 = x2
            f1 = f2
            x2 = lo + gr * (hi2 - lo)
            f2 = _coord_eval(x2, old, c, s, tmp, k2, base_cmax)
        if f1 < best_f:
            best_f, best_v = f1, float(x1)
        if f2 < best_f:
            best_f, best_v = f2, float(x2)

    for v in (0.0, hi, old):
        fv = _coord_eval(v, old, c, s, tmp, k2, base_cmax)
        if fv < best_f:
            best_f, best_v = fv, float(v)

    base_score = 2.0 * n * base_cmax
    if not np.isfinite(best_f) or best_f >= base_cmax * (1.0 - 1e-13):
        return None

    d = best_v - old
    if abs(d) > 1e-15:
        np.multiply(s, 2.0 * d, out=tmp)
        np.add(c, tmp, out=tmp)
        if 0 <= k2 < m:
            tmp[k2] += d * d
    else:
        tmp[:] = c

    q = 1.0 / (1.0 + d)
    c_new = tmp * (q * q)
    c_new = np.maximum(c_new, 0.0)

    a_new = a * q
    a_new[j] = best_v * q
    a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
    sn = float(a_new.sum())
    if sn <= 1e-12:
        return None
    a_new /= sn
    c_new /= (sn * sn)

    score_new = float(2.0 * n * c_new.max())
    if not np.isfinite(score_new) or score_new >= base_score - 1e-13:
        return None
    return a_new, c_new, score_new


def _pair_eval(d, c, diff, tmp, q1, q2, k, base_cmax):
    d = float(d)
    if abs(d) < 1e-15:
        return base_cmax
    np.multiply(diff, 2.0 * d, out=tmp)
    np.add(c, tmp, out=tmp)
    if 0 <= q1 < tmp.size:
        tmp[q1] += d * d
    if 0 <= q2 < tmp.size:
        tmp[q2] += d * d
    if 0 <= k < tmp.size:
        tmp[k] -= 2.0 * d * d
    return float(tmp.max())


def pair_line_search(a, c, j, l, n_evals=10):
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    j = int(j)
    l = int(l)
    if j == l or n < 2:
        return None
    old_j = float(a[j])
    old_l = float(a[l])
    if old_j <= 1e-15 or old_l <= 1e-15:
        return None

    base_cmax = float(c.max())
    diff = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    diff[j:j + n] = a
    diff[l:l + n] -= a

    lo = -old_j
    hi = old_l
    if hi <= lo + 1e-14:
        return None

    tmp = np.empty(m, dtype=np.float64)
    k = j + l
    q1 = 2 * j
    q2 = 2 * l

    grid = np.linspace(lo, hi, 16, dtype=np.float64)
    best_d = 0.0
    best_f = math.inf
    best_idx = 0
    for idx, d in enumerate(grid):
        f = _pair_eval(d, c, diff, tmp, q1, q2, k, base_cmax)
        if f < best_f:
            best_f = f
            best_d = float(d)
            best_idx = idx

    f0 = _pair_eval(0.0, c, diff, tmp, q1, q2, k, base_cmax)
    if f0 < best_f:
        best_f, best_d = f0, 0.0

    if best_idx <= 0:
        lo2 = lo
        hi2 = grid[min(1, len(grid) - 1)]
    elif best_idx >= len(grid) - 1:
        lo2 = grid[-2]
        hi2 = hi
    else:
        lo2 = grid[best_idx - 1]
        hi2 = grid[best_idx + 1]
    if hi2 <= lo2:
        lo2, hi2 = lo, hi

    gr = (math.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0
    x1 = hi2 - gr * (hi2 - lo2)
    x2 = lo2 + gr * (hi2 - lo2)
    f1 = _pair_eval(x1, c, diff, tmp, q1, q2, k, base_cmax)
    f2 = _pair_eval(x2, c, diff, tmp, q1, q2, k, base_cmax)
    if f1 < best_f:
        best_f, best_d = f1, float(x1)
    if f2 < best_f:
        best_f, best_d = f2, float(x2)

    for _ in range(max(2, int(n_evals) - 2)):
        if f1 < f2:
            hi2 = x2
            x2 = x1
            f2 = f1
            x1 = hi2 - gr * (hi2 - lo2)
            f1 = _pair_eval(x1, c, diff, tmp, q1, q2, k, base_cmax)
        else:
            lo2 = x1
            x1 = x2
            f1 = f2
            x2 = lo2 + gr * (hi2 - lo2)
            f2 = _pair_eval(x2, c, diff, tmp, q1, q2, k, base_cmax)
        if f1 < best_f:
            best_f, best_d = f1, float(x1)
        if f2 < best_f:
            best_f, best_d = f2, float(x2)

    for d in (lo, hi, 0.0):
        fd = _pair_eval(d, c, diff, tmp, q1, q2, k, base_cmax)
        if fd < best_f:
            best_f, best_d = fd, float(d)

    base_score = 2.0 * n * base_cmax
    if not np.isfinite(best_f) or best_f >= base_cmax * (1.0 - 1e-13):
        return None

    d = float(best_d)
    np.multiply(diff, 2.0 * d, out=tmp)
    np.add(c, tmp, out=tmp)
    if 0 <= q1 < m:
        tmp[q1] += d * d
    if 0 <= q2 < m:
        tmp[q2] += d * d
    if 0 <= k < m:
        tmp[k] -= 2.0 * d * d
    c_new = np.maximum(tmp, 0.0)

    a_new = a.copy()
    a_new[j] = max(0.0, old_j + d)
    a_new[l] = max(0.0, old_l - d)
    sn = float(a_new.sum())
    if sn <= 1e-12:
        return None
    a_new /= sn
    c_new /= (sn * sn)

    score_new = float(2.0 * n * c_new.max())
    if not np.isfinite(score_new) or score_new >= base_score - 1e-13:
        return None
    return a_new, c_new, score_new


def select_coordinates(a, c, top_k):
    n = a.size
    if n <= top_k:
        return np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)

    top_peaks = max(1, min(8, c.size))
    peaks = np.argsort(c)[-top_peaks:]
    peak_shift = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    for kk in peaks:
        k = int(kk)
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(k, n - 1)
        if hi >= lo:
            idx = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            mirror = k - idx
            peak_shift[idx] += a[mirror]

    rank = peak_shift + 0.15 * a
    order = np.argsort(rank)[::-1]
    top_a = np.argsort(a)[::-1]

    seen = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
    selected = []
    limit = int(top_k)
    for idx in order:
        i = int(idx)
        if not seen[i]:
            seen[i] = True
            selected.append(i)
            if len(selected) >= limit:
                break
    for idx in top_a:
        i = int(idx)
        if not seen[i]:
            seen[i] = True
            selected.append(i)
            if len(selected) >= int(1.5 * limit):
                break
    return np.asarray(selected[:int(1.5 * limit)], dtype=np.int64)


def select_pairs(a, c, max_pairs=30, top_peaks=5):
    n = a.size
    m = c.size
    pairs = []
    seen = set()
    if n < 2 or m < 3:
        return pairs

    peaks = np.argsort(c)[-max(1, min(top_peaks, m)):]
    for kk in peaks:
        k = int(kk)
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(k, n - 1)
        if hi < lo:
            continue
        idx = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        mirror = k - idx
        prod = a[idx] * a[mirror]
        order = np.argsort(prod)[::-1]
        for pos in order[:max(1, 2 * max_pairs)]:
            j = int(idx[pos])
            l = int(mirror[pos])
            if j == l:
                continue
            if j > l:
                j, l = l, j
            key = (j, l)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                pairs.append(key)
            if len(pairs) >= max_pairs:
                return pairs
    return pairs


def coordinate_descent(a_in, budget, t_start, max_top=400):
    a = normalize_sum1(a_in)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        c = conv_fft(a)
        return float(2.0 * n * c.max()), a

    budget = max(0.0, float(budget))
    deadline = t_start + budget
    if deadline - time.monotonic() < 8.0:
        c = conv_fft(a)
        return float(2.0 * n * c.max()), a

    c = conv_fft(a)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
    best_a = a.copy()

    if n > 8000:
        top_k = 60
        evals = 11
    elif n > 2500:
        top_k = 80
        evals = 12
    else:
        top_k = 100
        evals = 14

    stalls = 0
    sweep = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 6.0:
        sweep += 1
        c = conv_fft(best_a)
        cur = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
        if cur < best_score:
            best_score = cur
            best_a = normalize_sum1(best_a)

        top_k = min(int(max_top), n, top_k + 35)
        if n > 8000:
            evals = max(8, evals - 1)
        if deadline - time.monotonic() < 60.0:
            evals = max(7, evals - 2)

        improved = 0
        a = best_a.copy()
        cc = c.copy()

        max_pairs = min(30, max(8, top_k // 4))
        pairs = select_pairs(a, cc, max_pairs=max_pairs, top_peaks=5)
        for pair in pairs:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 6.0:
                break
            j, l = pair
            res = pair_line_search(a, cc, int(j), int(l), n_evals=max(6, evals - 3))
            if res is not None:
                a, cc, sc = res
                if sc < best_score - 1e-13:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    improved += 1

        coords = select_coordinates(a, cc, top_k)
        for j in coords:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 6.0:
                break
            res = coordinate_line_search(a, cc, int(j), n_evals=evals)
            if res is not None:
                a, cc, sc = res
                if sc < best_score - 1e-13:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    improved += 1

        if improved == 0:
            stalls += 1
            if stalls >= 3:
                break
        else:
            stalls = 0

    c = conv_fft(best_a)
    final_score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
    if final_score < best_score:
        best_score = final_score
    return best_score, best_a


def fallback_sequence():
    n = 64
    return [1.0 / n] * n


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(total) or total <= 0.0:
        total = 2400.0

    reserve = 35.0 if total > 120.0 else 0.15 * total
    search_deadline = t_start + max(5.0, total - reserve)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if int(seed) % 0 == 0 else 42)

    parent = None
    try:
        raw = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if raw is not None:
            arr = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if arr.size >= 16 and float(arr.sum()) > 1e-12:
                parent = normalize_sum1(arr)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        n0 = 4096
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi, n0)
        base = 1.0 + 0.25 * np.sin(x) ** 2 + 0.1 * np.cos(2.0 * x)
        parent = normalize_sum1(np.maximum(base, 1e-4))

    starts = [parent]
    for alpha in (-1.25, -0.6, 0.6, 1.25):
        starts.append(tilt_sequence(parent, alpha))
    if parent.size >= 1024 and parent.size != 4096:
        starts.append(resample_length(parent, 4096))
    if parent.size < 8192:
        starts.append(resample_length(parent, 8192))
    for st in (0.10, 0.22):
        starts.append(perturb_spectral(parent, rng, st))

    pool = []
    pool_scores = []

    best_score = math.inf
    best_a = parent.copy()

    for s in starts:
        try:
            sc, _ = conv_score_with_c(s)
        except Exception:
            sc = math.inf
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = np.asarray(s, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        append_candidate(pool, pool_scores, s, sc, limit=12)

    print(f"initial best internal={best_score:.8f} n={best_a.size} t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    remaining = search_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 60.0:
        p1 = min(300.0, max(60.0, remaining * 0.20))
        sc1, a1 = coordinate_descent(best_a, p1, time.monotonic(), max_top=300)
        if sc1 < best_score:
            best_score, best_a = sc1, a1
        append_candidate(pool, pool_scores, best_a, best_score, limit=12)
        print(f"phase1 coordinate internal={best_score:.8f} n={best_a.size} t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    remaining = search_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 120.0:
        adam_budget = min(remaining * 0.35, max(120.0, min(900.0, total * 0.30)))
        sc2, a2 = adam_lse_refine(best_a, adam_budget, time.monotonic())
        if sc2 < best_score:
            best_score, best_a = sc2, a2
        append_candidate(pool, pool_scores, best_a, best_score, limit=12)
        print(f"phase2 adam internal={best_score:.8f} n={best_a.size} t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    remaining = search_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 420.0:
        diverse_budget = min(220.0, remaining * 0.15)
        divers = []
        divers.append(tilt_sequence(best_a, 0.9))
        divers.append(tilt_sequence(best_a, -0.9))
        divers.append(perturb_spectral(best_a, rng, 0.18))
        if best_a.size < 11000:
            divers.append(resample_length(best_a, int(min(12000, max(1024, best_a.size * 1.12)))))
        elif best_a.size > 8000:
            divers.append(resample_length(best_a, 8192))

        per = max(25.0, diverse_budget / max(1, len(divers)))
        for d_a in divers:
            if time.monotonic() > search_deadline - 150.0:
                break
            scd, ad = coordinate_descent(d_a, per, time.monotonic(), max_top=160)
            if scd < best_score:
                best_score, best_a = scd, ad
            append_candidate(pool, pool_scores, best_a, best_score, limit=12)
            print(f"diverse candidate internal={best_score:.8f} n={best_a.size} t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    remaining = search_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 30.0:
        max_top = 500 if best_a.size < 5000 else 380
        sc3, a3 = coordinate_descent(best_a, remaining, time.monotonic(), max_top=max_top)
        if sc3 < best_score:
            best_score, best_a = sc3, a3
        append_candidate(pool, pool_scores, best_a, best_score, limit=12)
        print(f"phase3 final coordinate internal={best_score:.8f} n={best_a.size} t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    final_a = best_a
    final_internal = best_score

    if final_a is None or final_a.size == 0:
        final_a = np.asarray(fallback_sequence(), dtype=np.float64)
        final_internal = math.inf

    verify_deadline = t_start + max(0.0, total - 5.0)
    verified_best = None
    verified_score = math.inf

    candidates = []
    seen_arr = []
    for arr in [final_a]:
        candidates.append(np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64))
    if pool:
        order = np.argsort(pool_scores)
        for idx in order:
            if time.monotonic() > verify_deadline - 20.0:
                break
            candidates.append(pool[int(idx)])

    for arr in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > verify_deadline - 8.0:
            break
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size < 1:
            continue
        out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in arr]
        if len(out) == 0 or float(sum(out)) < 0.01:
            continue
        try:
            scv = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            scv = math.inf
        if np.isfinite(scv):
            if scv < verified_score:
                verified_score = scv
                verified_best = arr
            print(f"verified candidate internal~ n={arr.size} eval={scv:.8f} t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)
            if verified_score < 1.500001:
                break

    if verified_best is not None and verified_score < final_internal:
        final_a = verified_best
        final_internal = verified_score

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in final_a]
    if not out or float(sum(out)) < 0.01:
        out = fallback_sequence()

    try:
        final_check = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        if not np.isfinite(final_check):
            out = fallback_sequence()
            final_check = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        final_print = final_check
    except Exception:
        final_print = final_internal

    print(f"FINAL returned length={len(out)} internal={final_internal:.8f} final_eval={final_print:.8f} time={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)
    return out
```
